13/05/2026, 15:02 - Sleepless Zombiee: Future Founders Internship 2026 — inviting students from a select set of India's top engineering institutes.

This isn't a typical internship. It's an 8-week programme designed to give students a real taste of the founder experience: pick a Bharat-merchant problem, work in teams of 1-3, leverage the latest AI tools to build and ship a working product, and present it to an OkCredit panel. 

*Prize:*  The winning team takes ₹50,000 out of a ₹1.5 lakh prize pool, and every team that reaches the final milestone (winners included) gets a certificate of participation and completion that fits in a placement record. The programme brief has the full breakdown.

*Eligibility:*
Open to all
Any current student (UG or PG) at any college in India. Pre-final year priority.

Solo or team
Apply solo or in teams of up to 3. Lead invites teammates from the portal.

100% remote
No travel, no on-campus requirement. Submit weekly from anywhere.

IP retained
You keep your code. OkCredit gets a non-exclusive showcase licence only.

*Application link:* https://okcredit.in/finternship

*Application Deadline:* 15 May, Friday

Greetings from the Center for Teaching and Learning, National Institute of Technology Warangal.

We are pleased to inform you that the Centre for Teaching and Learning (CTL), in association with HITACHI Energy, is organizing a six-day holistic skill development program for girl students ( UG, PG and PhD) during 9-15 July 2026.

This programme is designed to empower young women through a balanced and engaging approach, focusing on:

Soft Skills Development
Mentoring and Life Skills
Self-Counseling and Emotional Well-being

The sessions aim to enhance confidence, emotional intelligence, and career readiness through interactive and experiential learning methods.

In this regard, we kindly request you to register for this program and enjoy the benefits. Also circulate the attached brochure among the girl students of our  institution and encourage them to participate in this programme.

We believe this initiative will significantly benefit you in your personal and professional development.
Find the registration link :  https://docs.google.com/forms/d/e/1FAIpQLSfqUCZcMQwgXv1meXDmgOnM1HJq8jyumixYVxWjUZjuf0IhaA/viewform?usp=header

For any further information, please feel free to contact us.
There is no Registration fee for the NIT Warangal girl students who are on rolls. Use this opportunity to build yourself. 

Thank you.
